## Receipt foundation

The previous individual writer validated start time and native identity/status bindings but could accept a 201 response without matching the confirmed end time. The end was inspected later by the host. An acknowledgement cannot safely become the success boundary until that gap is closed.

The candidate makes `endsAt` mandatory, rejects invalid/nonpositive/over-eight-hour intervals before quota/provider access, sends `ends_at`, and matches the acknowledged end as an instant. It retains exact patient/business/practitioner/type/marker/ID/active-state matching, bounded response parsing, process-local duplicate protection and response-loss uncertainty. Later observation also matches the interval; inspection cannot substitute a different expected end.

Standalone booking captures the approved offer's end. The earlier embedded Cliniko test host now requires a trusted end in its offer evidence and refuses duration drift between proposal and commit, while retaining its exclusive-permit and conflict-inspection contract. That host is not the transactional owning-diary implementation. The controlled historical probe callers also supply the explicit interval; no live probe was executed.

[ADR 0002](../docs/adr/0002-provider-acknowledged-booking.md), PRD, technical design, glossary and activation/booking notes distinguish the delivered receipt foundation from the still-proposed host success change. Existing D-16 history, other operation contracts, schema and phone-to-ID-only persistence are preserved. There are no new dependencies or settings.

## Individual booking runtime candidate

Runtime commit `86e09b444818356113af626379de5f165817bcf0` is published in draft PR #460 against `92a16e58b559dbd730ad8f0c50c4f23ecbe57bbb`. Its push run `38872610729` and PR run `38872618610` failed before job execution. Check `116880049725` independently reported the same billing/spending condition. Full local qualification subsequently completed successfully in the tracked `Abby individual complete local suite` user-systemd scope; its exit 0 and results were consumed without restarting after observation timeouts.

The stage 4 delta is based on receipt PR #459, including its separate fixture correction. `ClinikoBookingGuard.authorizeForSend` and concrete/catalogue offer dispatch paths finish proof, file/catalogue approval, SAM settings, shared write-stop checks and native metadata preparation before final availability. The POST budget is claimed before this path; unavailable quota refuses before sending, without a quota wait after the final observation. Online reads retain complete pagination. Roster dispatch retains its complete double calendar observation and performs no later metadata refresh; ordinary protected proposal/reschedule reads keep their existing refresh contract. Immediately before transport invocation, only synchronous original-expiry, local proof/lifetime and abort checks remain.

The individual host returns committed on the exact validated creation acknowledgement and performs no created-row/conflict GET or automatic review closure. A synchronous trusted acknowledgement notification propagates that established result through the host, reviewed-send wrapper and effect worker. The worker accepts only its matching booking action while its dispatch is still pending; late reports cannot reopen a finished dispatch, reports for other operations are ignored, and normal runtime action/version/lease acceptance still applies. Unparsed/held/lost/nonexact responses remain uncertain. No local durable acknowledgement/attempt record, automatic observer, timer extension, slot reservation or additional setting is introduced.

Catalogue, intake-to-booking and normal call-flow adapters forward the trusted notification. At stage 4, native class creation and the other operations retained their existing completion semantics. Product notes and ADR 0002 activated only the individual contract in that candidate; the separate class revision is recorded below. Class movement/pass relinking, rescheduling, cancellation, correction, resend and the earlier embedded/owning-diary contracts remain unchanged by the creation revisions. All thirteen gaps and external acceptance requirements remain in scope. Audit R11 starts with a small [product facts reference](PRODUCT_FACTS.md), kept separate from public FAQs/support advice; its content and 21 cited source/test/ADR files have matching fingerprints, without an automated behavioral or publication attestation.

`40a51f2f6c7182f394dee6b02c1fae66ce316171`. It is not landed or activated.

executed steps. Checks `116896272685` and `116896427317` independently reported
the account billing/spending condition. This establishes no code failure; no
unchanged workflow was rerun or account/repository policy changed to bypass it.
`ClinikoClassBookingHost` completes class permission, exact played consent,
approved pass/history, proof, metadata, policy/SAM/review and POST quota work
before the final complete double occurrence/attendance observation. The reader's
creation-only `inspectForSend` path leaves ordinary protected reads and movement
bracketing intact. Immediately before transport, synchronous original expiry,
local proof/lifetime and request checks remain; there is no intervening awaited
policy, patient, entitlement or quota work.

`ClinikoClassAttendanceWriter.executeBookingOnce` validates exact bounded native
HTTP 201 attendee/self, patient, occurrence, action marker, explicit active state
and requested case. It synchronously reports the receipt through the existing
host/review/worker result path. Later capacity/duplicate/pass-use races, observer
failure or cancellation cannot turn that established creation into uncertainty.
Lost, nonexact or unparsed responses still cannot confirm or replay. No post-create
GET, automatic compensation, observer job, review closure, extra setting,
dependency or standalone durable patient-bearing state was introduced.

Ordinary creation can acknowledge an omitted optional case property without
claiming no association. A returned unapproved case or undocumented null/empty
shape remains nonexact. A pass response must return its approved case. The official
OpenAPI bundle retrieved again October 16 matches the October 14 SHA256 recorded
in the class notes; it documents no concrete nil-link shape. Class moves still
use their separate native null-filter inspection for required unlinked staging.
Cancellation, movement, compensation and relinking use the existing generic
writer and their operation-specific case/effect inspection. Creation alone proves
neither payment nor atomic capacity/pass exclusion.

The docs/ADRs, product facts and scenario descriptions now reflect this scoped
creation revision. Manifest 1.227 retains all 418 scenarios and their full-call

## Composed dispatch lease correction

Runtime `94fd49e2ac5d250cf8fafb5d85d35a4d5b683370` is published in draft
[PR #462](https://github.com/garthtrickett/abby/pull/462), based on class `ae15000`.
Push run `38880747568` and PR run `38880804698` ended before execution; inspected
PR check `116906810004` reported account billing/spending limits with no job
steps. This supplies no code-failure verdict or successful cloud qualification.

Stage 6 source review found that booking preparation validated the current
session lease, but final synchronous dispatch checks retained only patient/offer
expiry and local proof/lifetime. A lease could expire during the last native read
while the proof and offer remained valid. Actual SAM reproduction in the
isolated `codex/abby-booking-dispatch-lease` worktree based on class `ae15000`
sent one POST in each expired online/roster/class case; their observed-renewal
controls passed. The initial six-case reproduction had three failures and three
passes, all without skips. This is a pre-send correctness gap, independent of
the later exact-acknowledgement success policy.

The correction retains the exact lease deadline from the last validated SAM
snapshot before final availability/capacity. Individual dispatch binds it to its
prepared request; the class owner holds its own captured deadline. The deadline
must remain live both when the native read returns and at transport invocation.
An already observed renewal supplies its newer deadline; an unobserved renewal
cannot extend this captured send window. No extra ledger/provider/policy read,
new timer, remote-stop replica, schema, dependency, setting or durable
patient-bearing state is introduced. Other operation contracts and established
acknowledgement results retain their semantics.

## Gap 11 provider selection

On October16 the owner selected Stripe Checkout after the provider recommendation.
The proposed integration uses a separate hosted Checkout Session for each approved
deposit/payment request, bound to the exact clinic account, action, amount,
currency and authorized recipient. Use verified payment evidence for paid
status; a completed browser redirect or delivered link is not payment proof.

does not restore a cancelled appointment.

## Refinement: startup admission, active lease and terms expiry

The next source review found two distinct expiry defects in the unpublished
foundation. The previous full typecheck pass remains evidence for its frozen
source, not this later revision.

First, the admission deadline bounds startup activation. Existing core
`sessionActivated`/`admissionExpired` transitions enforce it while the call is
starting; an active call instead follows its renewable session lease. The
foundation incorrectly also limited later phase consent by that old startup
deadline. A real admitted/activated/renewed-session regression failed before
correction, log `be9df097-1792149846165.log`. The refined test renews the same
session at 20, 40 and 60 seconds and checks consent at 65 seconds, after a
full quota window. The original startup deadline stays unchanged; ending or
lease expiry still denies consent, and no proof expiry or mutation is extended.
Malformed admission metadata remains refused.

`be9df097-1792149946152.log`. Phase consent is now also bounded by valid,
unexpired movement terms; proof/lease renewal cannot revive expired terms.
The original immutable terms and targets are retained. The actual native
caller and operator paths remain unfinished; no terms-refresh authority is
invented here.

`ClassMovementVerifier` owns actual rescheduling-verifier succession for one
private subject. It reuses a live proof; when expired, it invokes `renewOnce`
before a protected read can clear the old source. Dedicated private code entry
establishes only a fresh proof. It cannot redirect an old proof object, adopt
a different patient, reset exhausted answers or grant phase consent. An
interrupted held answer retires the successor and ignores late success.

five-minute proof expiry. No ordinary caller turn or durable queue carries
code/contact/proof values.

## Native staged caller routing: unpublished implementation checkpoint

Above local `e47e32c`, the native class movement host now keeps one private
staged execution and verifier for the call lifetime, with each advance still
using its own bounded native allocation and effect signal. The initial
`:write` guard authorizes only the replacement stage. Later phases validate
their actual consent-derived in-flight effect, played presentation, immutable
mutation, current owner reference/version/ordinal, current approval and native
permission. Ordinary `undefined` native case association is normalized only
to public `notApplicable`; pass association remains a separate stage. Only
joint inspected completion produces a moved receipt. Waiting rotates the
one-use continuation and does not replay an earlier send.

The native host issues fresh same-contract terms after re-reading the captured
source/destination/movement policies. The version, wording and movement targets
remain identical to the original immutable action; its original expiry is not
edited. The current live lease, proof, fresh policy expiry and two-minute
maximum bound the next confirmation, and replacement remains bound to its
original offer deadline. A changed quote or captured source is refused.

The private verifier now renews when the remaining proof cannot cover the
native boundary's required two-minute margin, as well as after expiry. It
retires the old owner and requires the original same-subject private-code
succession. Preparing a live protected readback checks the current verifier's
actual subject proof, including contact and patient permission. Non-disclosing
ownership metadata is confined to private routing guards. The earlier
metadata-only preparation was insufficient: two host tests reproduced a fresh
protected readback after contact or patient permission was withdrawn, log
`be9df097-1792154842009.log`. Restoring the actual proof check retires the
continuation and produces only a fixed review notice on refusal. The combined
proof/readback and verifier contracts then passed 31 cases, log
`be9df097-1792154864099.log`; the subsequent cost-drift, host, call-flow and voice
contracts passed 28 cases, log `be9df097-1792155014467.log`. These scopes overlap
and do not replace full qualification. Protected native boundaries also check
the current actual proof independently. Readback proof requests consume the
shared account budget before a later phase allocation; complete admission must
account for that real remaining capacity. Unknown callback costs remain
unknown; a request floor or physical cap is not a complete phase ceiling.

A shared `ClassMovementTurns` gateway and core reducer accept the caller's
continuation request once, check the current call/session/lease/policy/write
stop and revision, and prepare the exact target/terms/stage readback. The
private owner selects the stage; the model receives no stage-selection or
resume capability. Near-expiry renewal routes through private code entry;
code success prepares no send. Only a subsequent exact affirmative turn after
actual playback admits a distinct phase effect. The admitted call flow and
VoiceSession now delegate that effect to the existing owner. Closing the
owner retires its proof/native lifetime; serialized references cannot rebuild it.

The first actual host check failed after a real replacement because the
synthetic fixture lacked the required provider-owned review GET response,
log `be9df097-1792152602638.log`. Supplying the exact row/self link/update
version, without weakening the reader, passed seven host/verifier checks in
`be9df097-1792152648993.log`. The expanded 59-case run passed 57 and failed two
new test calls to nonexistent `speechBegan`; the existing runtime method is
`beginSpeech`. Correcting those fixture calls then passed all 14 staged host
cases in `be9df097-1792152760957.log`. The 40 existing ordinary/pass host cases
passed in the earlier 59-case run; this is not a clean 59-case aggregate claim.

floor never supplies an upper bound. Declarations describe trusted callback

revision adapter supplies a zero-native-request declaration; arbitrary revision

phase's180 ceiling and are not yet admitted.

### Cold staff movement recovery: unpublished memory checkpoint

A separate worktree/branch above native caller PR467 adds cold staff resolution
without reconstructing a caller owner, proof or confirmation. A current clinic
administrator and directory membership, independently investigated/quiescent
authority, exact captured policy/terms/staging and fresh joint native records
are required. An acknowledged review can yield distinct reviewed-moved or
reviewed-restored observations; the ended caller action is unchanged. The
reader makes only native GETs, never replays a mutation or persists a local
patient-linked resolution. Missing replacement evidence cannot establish
restoration from an empty active roster. Current policies/upcoming occurrences
bound this initial route; expired/different contracts and past occurrences
stay for manual Cliniko investigation. ADR0003 records this domain distinction.

The exact policy/terms digest and text generation is shared with the live
host through one contract helper, preserving the existing serialized version
and wording. Native reader/writer inspection and pass evidence are reused;
no weaker alternate native parser is added. A final joint class re-observation
rejects changed attendance after the exact record inspections. The private
staff read scope is freshly authenticated, expires/aborts independently, is
never returned or serialized and grants no caller or provider-write authority.

The optional authenticated dashboard hook exposes POST
`/api/class-movements/review` only when explicitly provisioned by the owning
host. Same-origin/admin checks, actor re-read after factory creation, exact
citation-only input and fixed output redaction precede the response. Setup-only
and unprovisioned surfaces return404. No new public setting activates it.

failure and four-file PostgreSQL reproductions later passed. Their timing
cause remains unproven. A separate deterministic held-read gateway test did
reproduce a source defect: the deadline aborted the native read and then the
final readiness check reused that already-aborted signal, preventing the
intended `bookingMenuFailure` notice from being recorded.

`be9df097-1792149262049.log`. The correction leaves the deadline and canceled
native read intact. After initial authority was established, only the generic
retry/fresh-options notice may cross the atomic current call/session/lease and
conversation-revision boundary at a mandatory trusted clock. It carries no
native selection, patient information or send authority. External abort takes
precedence; invalid or backwards time is refused. Positive evidence still
requires the original fresh readiness checks. No timeout can resume or replay
the read, and no staff/provider write is created.

### Synthetic caller clear-fence and reply boundary defect

During the still-running combined6a55 full suite1792171749450, the ordinary
staged signed journey failed157.39s at staged-runner line68, the Yes for original
cancellation, with socket3/child live and unavailable diagnostic state. Only the
initial code was issued; two provider mutations (review/replacement), one native
write outcome and120 clock ticks were recorded. The maximum-history staged
signed case passed271.80s in this combined run. The outer unmigrated shared
schema separately causes storage failures. No clean overall regression follows.
No active source, schema, assertion, deadline or concurrency was changed.

Source inspection found the shared synthetic compiled caller recorded outbound
marks but ignored clear messages. The actual stream emits a clear fence and
waits for its echo; the fixture can leave it unresolved until the normal2s
clear deadline. Also say/digit waited only for speech IPC, allowing an old
unacknowledged marker to precede the new response's actual wire mark. A
separate test helper first preserved the old behavior, and the real stream
clear-fence test failed false != true after2s, work1792173191681. The second
fresh-response boundary case passed. This establishes a fixture protocol defect;
it does not establish the ordinary journey failure's complete timing cause.

The isolated correction automatically echoes outstanding completion marks and
the final fence exactly once in wire order on clear. The real production stream
classifies these as ignored, never played. Explicit normal playback still needs
its own manual receipt. Say/digit retain their existing deadlines but await a
new wire marker as well as speech IPC; an older prompt cannot satisfy the
response boundary. No production consent, timer, admission or transport code
changed. Five pure protocol cases (including existing Twilio stream tests)
passed work1792173249345. New-source full TypeScript, affected signed journeys,
healthy-schema combined regression and cloud/review/clinic acceptance remain
open. The existing full run continues naturally and is not retried in parallel.

Adding the already-played-before-clear case and reviewed facts/registry yields
eight passing cases/zero skips, work1792173314782. No signed acceptance is
inferred from these pure wire and metadata checks.

A failure-only diagnostic now includes the numeric WebSocket close code and
playback counts/booleans. It excludes close reason text, mark names, patient

digest, exact intent recall, financial/native action UUIDs, created target, time

current public key and expected private intent. It is not financial permission,
received money, immutable history, durable retention or a replay grant. Keys are
injected only; no serving route, real provider/sender call, new dependency,
local patient-bearing record, deployment or activation is added. Actual host

## Native financial intent/observation retention candidate

`codex/abby-financial-record-retention` is stacked on draft492/c8d865d4. It adds
provider-owned prospective intent creation, signed native booking-observation
retention and an exact authenticated observation read. The owner-selected storage
boundary remains intact: Cliniko owns private pending finance context, Stripe
owns money/receipts, and Abby's existing barrier stores opaque consumed IDs plus
approved non-patient ownership metadata only. No payment state table is introduced.

The native client requires the barrier's exact scope/derived owner binding,
current financial-service permission, independent producer admission for POST,
and a final synchronous grant/lifetime guard. It does not reuse the caller's OTP
or treat a claim/signature as permission. The explicit review date is authorized
independently of the UTC fee deadline. Intent expiry/future creation closes before
native send; a valid later booking observation is still retention context, not a
payable grant. Writes validate exact201 record identity, patient/type links,
canonical family notes, marker, date, flags and timestamps. Readonly exact GET
rechecks current read permission after body processing and never admits effects.
Whole15-second/1MB bounds own late headers/body readers; there is no blind POST
retry, note update, raw upstream error/body exposure or public route.

Native record/query context and producer verification remain private memory;
only approved owner configuration contributes to the barrier binding. The same
physical native quota object is required. The client rejects an unbound barrier
or malformed truthy claim. Financial context/parser extraction preserves the
existing reader API and its20 checks. No dependencies or migrations are added.

The sender is a separate optional capability over the retained prospective request. It cannot reset an original delivery effect or turn a failed/unknown send into a retry. An exact current request has distinct record action, approval and message-effect IDs, canonical original intent/booking-observation/creation/Session references, purpose, sender/template selection digest and a finite expiry. The existing intent digest binds the original recipient, account, amount and booking. Native request/attempt content remains context, never independent approval or proof of money or delivery.

The concrete owner rereads the request, signed booking source, original financial review and actual Stripe account/Session. Independent current request and financial grants are mandatory alongside original message, source/history, exact provider-binding and producer/restore admission. An open unpaid unexpired Checkout permits only the current link; received money and a current Stripe-hosted receipt permit only the receipt. Changed recipient, selection, purpose, account, source, request, payment or approval refuses preparation or send. Bounded grants cannot continue after deadline, abort or consumer shutdown. Before any possible native attempt, permanent SQL exclusion consumes both the approved request's approval ID and the new send effect. Partial claims remain consumed. A distinct canonical native possible-resend attempt binds the exact request digest, approval/effect, sources, Session, purpose and payload digest. Its exact acknowledged retention and reread precede one real sender dispatch. Complete native history includes recalled/archived attempts and refuses replay after local exclusions disappear. A new attempt needs a new independently approved request and IDs; polling never mints them. Closing a review neither proves delivery nor restores permission. No new Checkout, refund, clinical mutation or restoration is performed.

The shared message protocol preserves the original public owner API and concrete first-delivery semantics. The resend owner checks8 measured post-effect calls plus an explicit configured maximum for extra authority calls before exclusion; this is headroom, not a quota reservation. Every actual call still claims the shared budget. Read-only request discovery has no producer or writable journal capability. Optional consumer provisioning requires both independent resend grants; absent provisioning is inactive and unresolved possible attempts remain for review.

## Gap12 independent native pending-recipient discovery,17October2026

The new `ClinikoOutboundPendingDiscovery.scanPending` is a bounded volatile read-only observation. Its explicit account-wide pending-read grant covers unrelated native rows because Cliniko documents DateTime/ID/patient filters rather than recall-type/notes filters. Per-record permission separately covers each exact private request and its decoded digest. No producer grant, writable journal, financial selection, SQL operation, audience/contact/DNC or launch permission is manufactured.

The scan uses only documented null-date filters, stable ascending IDs, at most500 account rows in five100-row pages and at most50 selected outbound requests. Stable totals, exact page size, self/patient/type/date/affirmative unrecalled/unarchived state and complete body decoding must agree. Every selected request is reread through the existing genuine read-only adapter and compared against its listed private context and native dates/closure. Changed records, duplicate/aliased action/approval/effect IDs, foreign bindings, role loss, unknown and oversized scans refuse without partial output. Multiple independent requests may share a campaign; a campaign ID is never an effect or contact approval. No completed/global-empty queue or snapshot atomicity is claimed.

The whole account/record grant, shared-budget, page/header/body and reread sequence shares one cancellation/deadline and a finite non-backwards trusted clock. Each physical GET consumes the supplied shared account budget; the existing180 limit is unchanged, without a new private budget or reservation. Fifty selected requests and the maximum500-row account scan are independently bounded; extra authority calls still share actual physical capacity. Methods/configuration are snapshotted and bound to their original receivers. Private context is returned only after final current authority checks, never to stdout or a local payload/retry file.

## Gap12 current clinic campaign-definition approval,18October2026

The new owner-file adapter reuses existing owner-only bounded JSON and current clinic-admin approval/membership checks. Closed schema rejects patient/recipient/DNC/notes fields and unsupported source/purpose, malformed policy/time/identity and out-of-bound capacity. `current(signal)` rereads current non-patient campaign approval before any audience loading; no patient/request seed is required. `currentFor(selection,request,signal)` additionally canonicalizes and snapshots the private native request and source selection before waits, then matches exact campaign/purpose/source/policy revision/fingerprint/schedule. A domain-separated non-patient fingerprint binds decoded configuration and captured approving membership, not a byte-level file checksum or approval supplied by the request itself. File/content/revision/provider changes, disabled/unapproved state, withdrawn/changed/non-admin membership, wrong scope, expiry, backwards time or cancelled/held observation refuse. Configuration, directory methods and clock are snapshotted/bound; source/read payloads are frozen. Existing owner-file permission/symlink/size checks stay intact.

An approved definition may be read before its contact window; time eligibility, exact audience membership and current contact/DNC remain independent. Policy/current file approval never supplies native record permission, patient authority, producer/complete restore, capacity, launch or clinical consent. No private queue, SQL migration, provider I/O, poller, scheduler, call launcher, callback/result or opt-out mutation is added. No actual clinic file/content/provider/contact activation is established.

## Explicit financial consumer in the owning voice runner, 18 October 2026

The reusable clinical runner now accepts an explicit `financialConsumer(context)` provision from its deploying host. This is independent of clinical call admission and supplies no financial grants from environment flags, catalogue settings or caller input. The context includes the shared native account budget and service signal. A supplied concrete owner must match the exact clinic scope, credential fingerprint and physical budget; construction-time identity snapshots cannot be rebound by later configuration edits. Independent financial source, collection, recipient, provider-binding, producer/restore and run authority remain required.

After standalone privacy and database/route preparation, the runner starts the provisioned owner and exposes its existing exact `/stripe/webhook` handler through the shared loopback HTTP listener. Raw bytes reach the independent Stripe verifier before Twilio body parsing; method, body/header bounds and authenticated current-money/native correlation retain their existing contracts. Clinical readiness is unchanged. The CLI creates no financial consumer by default. Service shutdown aborts and drains the owned consumer and raw route before closing the listener and database. The shared service signal is available for separately provisioned financial retention after a clinical acknowledgement; it is not a caller's cancellation signal.

## Conditional one-shot follow-up and portable capacity, 19 October 2026

`ClinikoOutboundLaunchOwner` composes the actual native request/contact/history
readers, current clinic campaign approval and four separate audience/provider/
producer/launch grants. It checks the due civil schedule, exact current phone,
contact/DNC lineage and complete possible-attempt history. A reviewed total native
cost uses the same physical account180 budget; headroom is never a reservation.
The v2 possible-attempt note retains the exact random capacity allocation ID
for independent cold recovery; legacy v1 evidence remains readable and still
prevents replay, but supplies no allocation link. Neither format proves a terminal
call. Permanent approval/effect claims and exact native acknowledgement precede one trusted provider adapter invocation. Its one-use callback
rechecks policy, native evidence, consent validity and all four current grants
immediately before transport. Exact acknowledgement means accepted, not delivered,

## Conditional terminal-provider observation and guarded release, 19 October 2026

The optional `ClinikoOutboundTerminalOwner` rereads actual native possible-attempt
context under current record grants, then separately requires native-source/mapping,
provider-observation and capacity-release grants. It uses the exact provider binding,
campaign and native v2 allocation. Dispatch metadata now carries both independent
effect and allocation IDs; changing the native allocation cannot borrow an earlier
call's terminal evidence. Legacy records/calls without the metadata stay unrecoverable
or unknown, not automatically released. Campaign cancellation does not prevent a
separately authorized terminal cleanup and grants no new launch.

The selected provider reader must establish authenticated complete terminal truth for
all possible matching calls. Active, absent, incomplete, foreign or malformed evidence
retains capacity. It is a trusted integration contract; no real carrier/backend reader
or public callback endpoint is installed. A callback body, clinic note, timeout, dead
process or expired lease alone cannot satisfy it. Terminal truth is volatile/provider-
owned, never a local patient or provider-call ledger. No contact delivery, opt-out or
clinical outcome is inferred.

The native record is read again after provider observation. The strongest decoded
native grant is retained per invocation, avoiding a shared mutable permission query
between concurrent inspections. Every independent grant, source record permission,
provider binding and deadline is checked again by a synchronous callback inside the
real SQL transaction, after advisory-lock/import-history waits and immediately before
release. A known SQL result survives later observer failure; a lost result stays unknown.
Permanent original exclusions remain consumed. Current source-origin/target ownership
mapping needs its own approval; old native context never authorizes an inferred mapping.

## Blocked-only Cliniko DNC retention foundation, 19 October 2026

The selected automatic opt-out policy now has a separate blocked-only native record adapter. `ClinikoOutboundDncRecords` accepts only canonical unknown-consent/blocked-DNC decisions with no reconsent approval, the exact selected contact-review type/content policy and the explicitly selected automation principal. It cannot grant consent, assert that an unverified participant withdrew the patient's personal consent, or pretend an automated decision was a human review. Its opaque owner binding contains only non-patient clinic/source/policy/principal selection. The existing permanent outbound effect barrier consumes only the independent record action; there is no new patient-bearing table, phone digest, report ledger, queue or reset/expiry API.

The current clinic-owned source definition, independently decoded record grant, current permission and complete producer/restore admission are separately required. The shared native transport bounds body/deadline/redirect behavior, uses the same physical account budget and rechecks permission/source/producer after waits and before its one native POST. Source withdrawal, expiry or lost current permission after the action claim prevents submission without restoring the action. Exact native acknowledgement is retained even if permission is later withdrawn; lost/malformed/foreign acknowledgements are unknown and consumed. An actual read-only factory needs no producer and retains exact closed/archived evidence without acquiring a writable capability.

The existing native contact lineage reader recognizes these blocked decisions; closure, archival or expiry cannot restore an older consenting head. Current source/read/head approval and any subsequent reconsent still require separate clinic authority. Configuring an automation principal or writing a note is not that approval. No report authentication, number-wide suppression or end-to-end automatic opt-out completion is inferred from this adapter. The provider owner must independently bind the exact call/number and authenticated report, apply clinic-approved automatic-write policy, handle unknown native acknowledgement through clinic/provider-owned durable truth and prevent every future call to a shared number. No patient verification, callback or clinical operation authority follows from a stop request.
